{"forms":[{"form":"dials","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The dial on the dashboard showed the car was nearly out of gas.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A graduated, circular scale over which a needle moves to show a measurement (such as speed)."]},{"glosses":["A graduated, circular scale over which a needle moves to show a measurement (such as speed).","Such a field as part of a clock face; (metonymic) the entire clock face."]},{"glosses":["A sundial."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Turn the dial to Radio 4: my favourite show is on!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A panel on a radio etc showing wavelengths or channels; a knob that is turned to change the wavelength etc."]},{"examples":[{"text":"His hands were too fat to operate the dial on the telephone.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A disk with finger holes on a telephone; used to select the number to be called."]},{"glosses":["A person's face."],"tags":["Australia","UK","slang"]},{"glosses":["A miner's compass."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈdaɪ.əl/","tags":["UK","US"]},{"ipa":"[ˈdaɪ.əɫ]","tags":["UK","US"]}],"upstream_line":174693,"upstream_offset":824793652,"upstream_record_sha256":"5028c44fd4c40c3ec0dcbebcb38f26c2f00f533500b6d8cde9cd5eb51748ab8c","word":"dial"}
{"forms":[{"form":"dials","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"dialing","tags":["US","participle","present"]},{"form":"dialling","tags":["UK","participle","present"]},{"form":"dialed","tags":["US","participle","past"]},{"form":"dialed","tags":["US","past"]},{"form":"dialled","tags":["UK","participle","past"]},{"form":"dialled","tags":["UK","past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The lead guitarist for the rock band Spinal Tap dialed his amplifier to 11.","type":"example"},{"text":"The president has recently dialled down the rhetoric.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To control or select something with a dial, or (figuratively) as if with a dial."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"In an emergency dial 999.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To select a number on a telephone, regardless of a physical dial."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To call (a person or organization) using a telephone."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Please be careful when dialling.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To use a dial or a telephone."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The application failed when attempting to dial the Postgres server, because there were too many open connections.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To initiate a connection to a remote computer service such as a database."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I always check my guestbook when I dial, just in case anyone saw my Web site.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To use a dial-up modem to connect a personal computer to the Internet."],"tags":["dated","intransitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈdaɪ.əl/","tags":["UK","US"]},{"ipa":"[ˈdaɪ.əɫ]","tags":["UK","US"]}],"upstream_line":174694,"upstream_offset":824815213,"upstream_record_sha256":"fc218422bb9fa9072284a83d71628e868a3817c0b29c02c8e91912e9b8acb6b0","word":"dial"}
